Este FRD alimenta diretamente o `05-sdd.md` (lente SWEBOK Design).\n>\n> **ORO triplet deste FRD** \u2014 Operator: MCORCH Master Execution Agent + L1 engineer \u00b7 Reviewer: Sovereign (Gabriel) \u00b7 Owner: Sovereign at\u00e9 v6.x (blast radius = persona p\u00fablica sint\u00e9tica + artefatos biom\u00e9tricos per-tenant + risco contratual BYOK por provider; migra a marketing-growth quando a North-Star de marca for definida e atingida \u2014 OTD-AC-013).\n>\n> **Decis\u00f5es travadas que este FRD respeita (n\u00e3o reabre):** (1) postura H\u00cdBRIDO rosto-real + avatar-backfill declarado (N\u00c3O 100%-avatar \u2014 FM-AC-014); (2) posicionamento GLOBAL ENGLISH-FIRST (1 trilha-fonte EN + auto-dub nativo da plataforma p/ PT-BR como alcance; voz PT clonada + re-lip-sync como premium); (3) avatar = m\u00f3dulo per-user BYOK PURO Modelo A no Canvas Studio \u2014 nunca conta-mestra multiplexada (service bureau proibido); (4) reply engine do X = escopo core; (5) idioma + frequ\u00eancia = per-user opt-in; (6) Gabriel AI = inst\u00e2ncia-zero B2B2C; (7) monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o h\u00edbrida mcoCoins 4\u00d7-floor por render via RPC `deduct_mco_coins` + planos; (8) LGPD biom\u00e9trico Art. 11 + EU AI Act Art. 50.\n>\n> **Constraints Harness n\u00e3o-negoci\u00e1veis aplicados neste FRD:** l\u00f3gica em hooks (hook-first; p\u00e1ginas/componentes display-only); billing SEMPRE via RPC `deduct_mco_coins` (nunca client-side); n\u00f3s do Canvas seguem o registry existente (factory + handles + inspector + nodeTypes map + resolveExecutePayload); toda tabela nova nasce com RLS default-deny + trigger `embed-mcorch-node` + isolamento tenant `user_id`(+`project_id`); Edge Function com JWT validation; artefatos biom\u00e9tricos (`avatar_identities`/`voice_profiles`/`avatar_consents`) = RLS own + storage isolado per-tenant + erase RPC (molde `erase_vision_artifacts`); SDD/FRD lista apenas o **NOME** da vault key, nunca o valor do secret.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Scope Statement\n\nEste documento especifica os requisitos funcionais (FR-AC) e n\u00e3o-funcionais (NFR-AC) do m\u00f3dulo `avatar-clone-ai` \u2014 o est\u00fadio de persona-IA h\u00edbrida per-user no Canvas Studio que clona rosto + voz + \"c\u00e9rebro\" (RAG sobre a memory mesh) e alimenta a cad\u00eancia de 30 dias sobre o Viral Autopilot, com conformidade biom\u00e9trica (LGPD Art. 11) e de disclosure (EU AI Act Art. 50 / CONAR) cravadas como gates n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1veis. Cada FR-AC decomp\u00f5e uma ou mais Product Requirements (PR-AC-001..028 do `03-prd.md`) em comportamento implement\u00e1vel e verific\u00e1vel, com categoria de implementa\u00e7\u00e3o e ID de teste de aceite.\n\n**In scope:**\n- M\u00f3dulo Avatar per-user BYOK PURO (Modelo A) com 5 n\u00f3s tipados no Canvas Studio: `avatarIdentity`, `voiceClone`, `avatarScript`, `talkingHead`, `avatarEnhance`.\n- Pipeline de s\u00edntese: identity-lock \u2192 Identity Gate (ArcFace, limiar calibrado) \u2192 voz clonada bil\u00edngue \u2192 roteiro c\u00e9rebro-RAG \u2192 talking-head 9:16 \u2192 enhance final \u2192 C2PA + disclosure \u2192 `creative_assets` \u2192 Viral Autopilot.\n- Infraestrutura biom\u00e9trica: `avatar_identities`, `voice_profiles`, `avatar_consents` (RLS own default-deny + storage isolado per-tenant + erase/retention RPC).\n- Compliance first-class: consentimento expl\u00edcito por finalidade + consentimento por-render fail-closed + identity-verification self-clone + disclosure/C2PA gate n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1vel.\n- Reply engine real-time do X (escopo core): poll mentions \u2192 reply do c\u00e9rebro-clonado \u2192 post com cap de gasto per-user.\n- Jun\u00e7\u00e3o com Viral Autopilot: relaxar `autopilot_plans.platforms` CHECK + `AVATAR_FLOW_PLATFORMS` + step `avatar_generation` independente + cad\u00eancia 30d com ramp por-canal.\n- Cost model `AVATAR_VIDEO_COST` separado (4\u00d7-floor) via RPC `deduct_mco_coins`; mapeamento a planos Starter/Pro/Enterprise.\n\n**Out of scope (Won't Have this cycle \u2014 `03-prd.md` \u00a74):**\n- Motor PR\u00d3PRIO soberano de talking-head (OTD-AC-001 h\u00edbrido futuro deferido).\n- Partnership HeyGen (OAuth Intake / MSA Enterprise \u2014 self-serve multi-tenant proibido no ToS).\n- Pesos self-host non-commercial (InfiniteYou / F5-TTS / FLUX.1-dev / XTTS-v2 / Fish-Speech \u2014 FM-AC-001).\n- Conte\u00fado eleitoral/candidato BR (TSE \u2014 FM-AC-009).\n- Auto-dub via API (controle exclusivo da YouTube Studio no browser \u2014 limite conhecido, n\u00e3o wirar via Data API).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n| ID | Category | Requirement | Target | Measurement |\n|----|----------|-------------|--------|-------------|\n| NFR-AC-001 | Performance | Canvas Studio page load (LCP) com n\u00f3s avatar | < 2.5s | Lighthouse CI |\n| NFR-AC-002 | Performance | `generate-voice` p95 latency (s\u00edntese \u2264900 chars) | < 8s | Supabase logs |\n| NFR-AC-003 | Performance | Identity Gate cosine check por frame | < 150ms/frame | Bench in-job |\n| NFR-AC-004 | Performance | `talkingHead` render (60s 9:16) \u00e9 ass\u00edncrono \u2014 UI nunca bloqueia; progresso via webhook/poll | UI responsiva \u2264 200ms ack; render off-band | Async ack log |\n| NFR-AC-005 | Scalability | Renders concorrentes por tenant sem degrada\u00e7\u00e3o (daily-cap respeitado) | \u2265 30 renders/dia/tenant dentro do cap | Load test |\n| NFR-AC-006 | Scalability | Reply engine poll loop respeita rate limits sem throttle | \u2264 300 mentions/15min + \u2264 100 replies/15min por user | X API headers |\n| NFR-AC-007 | Availability | Pipeline avatar (render\u2192finalize) uptime SLO | 99.5%/m\u00eas | Watchdog `infra_health_logs` |\n| NFR-AC-008 | Availability | Erase/retention RPC executa fail-open com reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o (artefato \u00f3rf\u00e3o self-heal) | 100% das revoga\u00e7\u00f5es eventualmente consistentes \u2264 24h | Sweep log + zero-residue audit |\n| NFR-AC-009 | Maintainability | L\u00f3gica de neg\u00f3cio em hooks; complexidade ciclom\u00e1tica por hook | \u2264 10 | ESLint |\n| NFR-AC-010 | Maintainability | N\u00f3s seguem o registry existente (factory+handles+inspector+nodeTypes+resolveExecutePayload) \u2014 zero diverg\u00eancia de padr\u00e3o | 100% dos 5 n\u00f3s conformes | Code review |\n| NFR-AC-011 | Security | Toda Edge Function user-facing exige JWT v\u00e1lido; cron/service-role usa `SB_SECRET_KEY` | 100% | `/security-review` |\n| NFR-AC-012 | Security | BYOK fail-closed: zero `Deno.env.get('<provider>')` em fluxo user-facing; SDD lista s\u00f3 NOME da vault key | 0 viola\u00e7\u00f5es | grep audit + `/security-review` |\n| NFR-AC-013 | Security | RLS cross-tenant: tenant A nunca l\u00ea artefato biom\u00e9trico de B | 0 leaks | RLS test (FMEA-011) |\n| NFR-AC-014 | Privacy/Compliance | 100% dos renders/posts carregam C2PA machine-readable + disclosure deepfake (gate n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1vel) | 100% | Publish gate audit |\n| NFR-AC-015 | Privacy/Compliance | 100% das cria\u00e7\u00f5es de avatar com `avatar_consents` imut\u00e1vel + identity-verification passada | 100% | `avatar_consents` query |\n| NFR-AC-016 | **Identity Fidelity** | Identity Gate pass-rate na 1\u00aa gera\u00e7\u00e3o (limiar calibrado FAR/FRR/EER, N\u00c3O hardcode); avatar uncanny descartado | \u2265 85% pass-rate (recalibr\u00e1vel) | Identity Gate log + Vision QA |\n| NFR-AC-017 | **Identity Fidelity** | Voz clonada sem code-switching PT-BR\u2194EN intra-frase nem accent-bleed (FM-AC-013) | 0 ocorr\u00eancias de code-switch intra-frase | A/B voz + QA manual |\n| NFR-AC-018 | **Render Determinism** | Render-core MP4 9:16 1080\u00d71920 sha256-id\u00eantico para mesma entrada (NFR-VS-016), exceto pelo waiver C2PA documentado (OTD-AC-008) | sha256 id\u00eantico OU waiver registrado | Render bench + spike FR-AC-037 |\n| NFR-AC-019 | Cost Integrity | `AVATAR_VIDEO_COST` \u2260 `VIDEO_COST`; dedu\u00e7\u00e3o SEMPRE at\u00f4mica via RPC `deduct_mco_coins`; zero dedu\u00e7\u00e3o client-side | 0 desvios | Ledger audit |\n| NFR-AC-020 | Observability | Cada path (success/degraded/error) emite telemetry em `infra_health_logs` (`service='avatar-clone-ai'`) + n\u00f3 de observa\u00e7\u00e3o na Knowledge Mesh no 1\u00ba sucesso | 100% dos paths | `infra_health_logs` query |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Interface Requirements\n\n### 4.1 UI \u2192 Hook \u2192 Edge Function Contract\n\n```\n[Component: AvatarStudioCanvas / BYOKSettingsCard / ConsentWizard]\n  \u2193 calls\n[Hook: useAvatarProviders / useAvatarConsent / useAvatarRender]\n  \u2193 calls (for sensitive ops)\n[Edge Function: generate-voice / canvas-execute (talkingHead) / erase-avatar-artifacts / avatar-reply-engine]\n  \u2193 reads/writes\n[Supabase Tables: user_api_keys (Vault) \u00b7 avatar_identities \u00b7 voice_profiles \u00b7 avatar_consents \u00b7 video_renders \u00b7 creative_assets \u00b7 channel_variants]\n```\n\nDisciplina Harness: componentes display-only; toda muta\u00e7\u00e3o/fetch nos hooks; sensitive ops (s\u00edntese, billing, erasure, reply) s\u00f3 em Edge Functions JWT-gated.\n\n### 4.2 Internal Edge Function Contracts\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/generate-voice` (FR-AC-009)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  script: string,            // input_script (texto do roteiro)\n  language: \"en\" | \"pt-BR\",  // idioma \u00fanico (nunca code-switch \u2014 FR-AC-010)\n  provider: \"elevenlabs\" | \"cartesia\",\n  voice_profile_id: string,  // refer\u00eancia a voice_profiles (owner-scoped)\n  project_id?: string,\n}\n```\n**Response (200):**\n```typescript\n{\n  audio_url: string,         // signed URL curta (bucket privado)\n  creative_asset_id: string, // registrado em creative_assets\n  mco_charged: number,       // ~36 (recalibr\u00e1vel)\n}\n```\n**Error codes:**\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 401 | Missing or invalid JWT |\n| 402 | `elevenlabs_not_configured` / `cartesia_not_configured` (BYOK fail-closed) OU saldo mcoCoins insuficiente |\n| 403 | Consentimento de voz revogado (fail-closed) ou sentinel block |\n| 422 | Valida\u00e7\u00e3o (idioma misto / script vazio \u2014 mensagem PT-BR no body) |\n| 500 | Internal error (logged to `infra_health_logs`) |\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/canvas-execute` (kind=`talkingHead`) (FR-AC-020/021)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  kind: \"talkingHead\",\n  input_face_identity: string,   // avatar_identities ref (owner-scoped)\n  input_voice_profile: string,   // audio asset ref\n  input_script: string,\n  source_type: \"footage_real\" | \"avatar_ai\",  // dirige roteamento de motor (FR-AC-022)\n  engine?: \"hedra\" | \"omnihuman\" | \"kling\",\n  reference_image_urls?: string[],\n  seed?: number,\n}\n```\n**Response (200 / async):**\n```typescript\n{\n  render_id: string,         // video_renders row\n  status: \"queued\",\n  transport: \"poll://\",      // webhook/poll async (NFR-AC-004)\n  mco_estimate: number,      // 480 Hedra / 2169 OmniHuman / 36 footage-real\n}\n```\n**Error codes:** 401 \u00b7 402 (`<engine>_not_configured` / saldo) \u00b7 403 (consent revogado / 100%-avatar sem footage real \u2014 FR-AC-033) \u00b7 422 \u00b7 500.\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/erase-avatar-artifacts` (FR-AC-027)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope) \u2014 tenant-guarded\n**Request:** `{ avatar_identity_id?: string, voice_profile_id?: string, reason: \"revoke\" | \"retention_sweep\" }`\n**Response (200):** `{ erased: { sql: number, storage: number, third_party: string[] }, attestation_log_id: string }`\n**Error codes:** 401 \u00b7 403 (tenant mismatch) \u00b7 404 \u00b7 500. Notifica\u00e7\u00e3o a terceiros (Art. 18) \u00e9 best-effort com reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o (NFR-AC-008).\n\n### 4.3 External BYOK Provider Contracts (Modelo A \u2014 per-user, fail-closed)\n\n> SDD/FRD lista apenas o **NOME** da vault key, nunca o valor. Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o per-user `auth.uid()`; aus\u00eancia \u2192 HTTP 402 estruturado. Veredito de pass-through por provider e cl\u00e1usula-\u00e2ncora em `00c \u00a7F1`.\n\n| Provider | Vault key (per-user) | Auth header | Endpoint-\u00e2ncora | Custo-\u00e2ncora (datado) | Veredito BYOK (Modelo A) |\n|----------|----------------------|-------------|-----------------|------------------------|--------------------------|\n| **Hedra** (Character-3, talking-head default) | `hedra_api_key` | provider API key | `api.hedra.com` (6 cr\u00e9d/s) | ~$0,0333/s = ~$2,00/min \u2192 **480 mco/60s** | CINZA-RESTRITIVO (\u00a71.3(c) \"service bureau or otherwise\"; BYOK puro escapa) |\n| **fal.ai / OmniHuman 1.5** (re-lip-sync footage-real) | `omnihuman_api_key` (\u2261 `fal_api_key`) | `Authorization: Key <key>` | `fal.ai/models/fal-ai/bytedance/omnihuman/v1.5` | $0,16/s \u2192 **2169 mco/60s** | CINZA (\u00a74(b)(ii) \"not expose APIs to End Users\"; user = Customer da fal) |\n| **Higgsfield** (Soul ID identity-lock / Kling avatar) | `higgsfield_api_key` (j\u00e1 existe) | provider API key | `higgsfield.ai` | Starter $15/200cr (op exato `n\u00e3o-verificado` \u2014 motor opt-in) | PERMITIDO-com-ressalva (\u00a74.4 libera Outputs comerciais) |\n| **ElevenLabs** (voz, PVC/IVC) | `elevenlabs_api_key` | `xi-api-key: <key>` | `api.elevenlabs.io` | ~$0,182/1k chars (V2) \u2192 **36 mco/900 chars** | PERMITIDO em BYOK / PROIBIDO conta-mestra |\n| **Cartesia** (voz real-time Sonic) | `cartesia_api_key` | provider API key | `api.cartesia.ai` (40-90ms) | tier-dependente (uso comercial exige tier do user) | CINZA \u2014 exige tier comercial do user (atestado FR-AC-005) |\n| **Magnific/Freepik** (enhance final) | `MAGNIFIC_ORG_API_KEY` (**org-key, N\u00c3O per-user**) | `x-magnific-api-key` | `api.magnific.com` (server-to-server) | ~$0,08/2K \u00b7 ~$0,16/4K | conta-org Business INTERNA (per-user PROIBIDO sem consent escrito \u2014 FICA DE FORA do BYOK) |\n| **HeyGen** | \u2014 (ausente) | \u2014 | \u2014 | \u2014 | PARTNERSHIP OBRIGAT\u00d3RIA (fora do MVP/v1) |\n| **X / Twitter** (reply engine) | `social_credentials.access_token` (per-user OAuth) | OAuth 2.0 Bearer | `api.x.com/2/users/:id/mentions` \u00b7 `POST /2/tweets` | read $0,005 \u00b7 reply $0,015 \u00b7 $0,20-com-link | pay-per-use, cap per-user (FR-AC-044) |\n\n**Resolution order (mandatory, API Tenancy Model):** (1) per-user table (`user_api_keys`/`social_credentials` por `auth.uid()`, `is_active=true`); (2) global vault fallback S\u00d3 em cron/service-role/onboarding default (nunca em fluxo user-facing de s\u00edntese); (3) hard failure \u2192 HTTP 402/501 estruturado. Magnific \u00e9 exce\u00e7\u00e3o declarada (org-key interna como custo do MCORCH, n\u00e3o pass-through).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Requirements\n\n> Toda tabela nova nasce com RLS own (`auth.uid() = user_id`) default-deny + trigger `embed-mcorch-node` + isolamento tenant. Valida\u00e7\u00e3o de campos com mensagem de erro em **Portugu\u00eas (Brasil)**.\n\n### 5.1 `avatar_identities`\n\n| Field | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|\n| id | uuid (PK) | No | gen default | \u2014 |\n| user_id | uuid (FK auth.users) | No | `= auth.uid()` (RLS) | \"Acesso negado a este artefato biom\u00e9trico.\" |\n| project_id | uuid | Yes | tenant scope | \u2014 |\n| persona_type | text | No | `IN ('self_clone','fictional')` | \"Tipo de persona inv\u00e1lido.\" |\n| tier | smallint | No | `IN (1,2,3)` | \"Tier de identidade inv\u00e1lido (1-3).\" |\n| identity_storage_key | text | No | bucket privado, prefixo `user_id/` | \"Chave de storage biom\u00e9trico inv\u00e1lida.\" |\n| arcface_threshold | numeric | No | calibrado (FAR/FRR/EER); N\u00c3O hardcode 0.95/0.7-0.8 | \"Limiar do Identity Gate n\u00e3o calibrado.\" |\n| verification_passed | boolean | No | default false; s\u00f3 true ap\u00f3s identity-verification gate | \"Verifica\u00e7\u00e3o de identidade pendente.\" |\n| created_at | timestamptz | No | default now() | \u2014 |\n\n### 5.2 `voice_profiles`\n\n| Field | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|\n| id | uuid (PK) | No | gen default | \u2014 |\n| user_id | uuid (FK) | No | `= auth.uid()` (RLS) | \"Acesso negado a este perfil de voz.\" |\n| project_id | uuid | Yes | tenant scope | \u2014 |\n| provider | text | No | `IN ('elevenlabs','cartesia')` | \"Provider de voz inv\u00e1lido.\" |\n| voiceprint_vault_ref | text | No | cifrado no Vault (n\u00e3o valor em claro) | \"Refer\u00eancia de voiceprint inv\u00e1lida.\" |\n| language | text | No | `IN ('en','pt-BR')` (sem code-switch) | \"Idioma de voz inv\u00e1lido ou misto.\" |\n| created_at | timestamptz | No | default now() | \u2014 |\n\n### 5.3 `avatar_consents` (imut\u00e1vel)\n\n| Field | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|\n| id | uuid (PK) | No | gen default | \u2014 |\n| user_id | uuid (FK) | No | `= auth.uid()` (RLS) | \"Acesso negado a este consentimento.\" |\n| purpose | text | No | `IN ('train_lora','face_embedding','voice_clone')` (checkbox separado) | \"Finalidade de consentimento inv\u00e1lida.\" |\n| term_version | text | No | vers\u00e3o do termo vigente | \"Vers\u00e3o do termo ausente.\" |\n| consent_granted | boolean | No | true s\u00f3 com checkbox espec\u00edfico | \"Consentimento espec\u00edfico ausente (LGPD Art. 11).\" |\n| ip_address | inet | No | IP do titular no consentimento | \"IP de consentimento ausente.\" |\n| revoked_at | timestamptz | Yes | null = ativo; preenchido = fail-closed | \u2014 |\n| created_at | timestamptz | No | default now() (linha imut\u00e1vel \u2014 sem UPDATE de conte\u00fado) | \u2014 |\n\n### 5.4 Reused / extended tables\n\n| Table | Change | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|--------|-----------------|----------------------|\n| `user_api_keys` | + colunas cifradas `hedra_api_key`/`omnihuman_api_key`/`elevenlabs_api_key`/`cartesia_api_key` (encrypt trigger + masked + INSTEAD OF) | per-user, `is_active` | \"Credencial do provider n\u00e3o configurada.\" |\n| `video_renders` | + `composition.avatar` jsonb + `engine IN ('hedra','omnihuman','kling','hyperframes')` | `AVATAR_VIDEO_COST` separado | \"Saldo de mcoCoins insuficiente.\" |\n| `creative_assets` | `source_module='avatar-studio'` + `parent_asset_id` (versionamento) | owner-scoped | \"Asset n\u00e3o pertence ao tenant.\" |\n| `autopilot_plans` | relaxar CHECK `platforms` p/ +youtube/tiktok/instagram/pinterest | FK composta tenant | \"Canal n\u00e3o permitido no plano.\" |\n| `channel_variants` | `asset_key` owner-scoped p/ avatar; campos de script | idempot\u00eancia UNIQUE | \"Variante duplicada para o canal.\" |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Scope Statement\n\nEste documento especifica os requisitos funcionais (FR-AC) e n\u00e3o-funcionais (NFR-AC) do m\u00f3dulo `avatar-clone-ai` \u2014 o est\u00fadio de persona-IA h\u00edbrida per-user no Canvas Studio que clona rosto + voz + \"c\u00e9rebro\" (RAG sobre a memory mesh) e alimenta a cad\u00eancia de 30 dias sobre o Viral Autopilot, com conformidade biom\u00e9trica (LGPD Art. 11) e de disclosure (EU AI Act Art. 50 / CONAR) cravadas como gates n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1veis. Cada FR-AC decomp\u00f5e uma ou mais Product Requirements (PR-AC-001..028 do `03-prd.md`) em comportamento implement\u00e1vel e verific\u00e1vel, com categoria de implementa\u00e7\u00e3o e ID de teste de aceite.\n\n**In scope:**\n- M\u00f3dulo Avatar per-user BYOK PURO (Modelo A) com 5 n\u00f3s tipados no Canvas Studio: `avatarIdentity`, `voiceClone`, `avatarScript`, `talkingHead`, `avatarEnhance`.\n- Pipeline de s\u00edntese: identity-lock \u2192 Identity Gate (ArcFace, limiar calibrado) \u2192 voz clonada bil\u00edngue \u2192 roteiro c\u00e9rebro-RAG \u2192 talking-head 9:16 \u2192 enhance final \u2192 C2PA + disclosure \u2192 `creative_assets` \u2192 Viral Autopilot.\n- Infraestrutura biom\u00e9trica: `avatar_identities`, `voice_profiles`, `avatar_consents` (RLS own default-deny + storage isolado per-tenant + erase/retention RPC).\n- Compliance first-class: consentimento expl\u00edcito por finalidade + consentimento por-render fail-closed + identity-verification self-clone + disclosure/C2PA gate n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1vel.\n- Reply engine real-time do X (escopo core): poll mentions \u2192 reply do c\u00e9rebro-clonado \u2192 post com cap de gasto per-user.\n- Jun\u00e7\u00e3o com Viral Autopilot: relaxar `autopilot_plans.platforms` CHECK + `AVATAR_FLOW_PLATFORMS` + step `avatar_generation` independente + cad\u00eancia 30d com ramp por-canal.\n- Cost model `AVATAR_VIDEO_COST` separado (4\u00d7-floor) via RPC `deduct_mco_coins`; Non-Functional Requirements\n\n| ID | Category | Requirement | Target | Measurement |\n|----|----------|-------------|--------|-------------|\n| NFR-AC-001 | Performance | Canvas Studio page load (LCP) com n\u00f3s avatar | < 2.5s | Lighthouse CI |\n| NFR-AC-002 | Performance | `generate-voice` p95 latency (s\u00edntese \u2264900 chars) | < 8s | Supabase logs |\n| NFR-AC-003 | Performance | Identity Gate cosine check por frame | < 150ms/frame | Bench in-job |\n| NFR-AC-004 | Performance | `talkingHead` render (60s 9:16) \u00e9 ass\u00edncrono \u2014 UI nunca bloqueia; progresso via webhook/poll | UI responsiva \u2264 200ms ack; render off-band | Async ack log |\n| NFR-AC-005 | Scalability | Renders concorrentes por tenant sem degrada\u00e7\u00e3o (daily-cap respeitado) | \u2265 30 renders/dia/tenant dentro do cap | Load test |\n| NFR-AC-006 | Scalability | Reply engine poll loop respeita rate limits sem throttle | \u2264 300 mentions/15min + \u2264 100 replies/15min por user | X API headers |\n| NFR-AC-007 | Availability | Pipeline avatar (render\u2192finalize) uptime SLO | 99.5%/m\u00eas | Watchdog `infra_health_logs` |\n| NFR-AC-008 | Availability | Erase/retention RPC executa fail-open com reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o (artefato \u00f3rf\u00e3o self-heal) | 100% das revoga\u00e7\u00f5es eventualmente consistentes \u2264 24h | Sweep log + zero-residue audit |\n| NFR-AC-009 | Maintainability | L\u00f3gica de neg\u00f3cio em hooks; complexidade ciclom\u00e1tica por hook | \u2264 10 | ESLint |\n| NFR-AC-010 | Maintainability | N\u00f3s seguem o registry existente (factory+handles+inspector+nodeTypes+resolveExecutePayload) \u2014 zero diverg\u00eancia de padr\u00e3o | 100% dos 5 n\u00f3s conformes | Code review |\n| NFR-AC-011 | Security | Toda Edge Function user-facing exige JWT v\u00e1lido; cron/service-role usa `SB_SECRET_KEY` | 100% | `/security-review` |\n| NFR-AC-012 | Security | BYOK fail-closed: zero `Deno.env.get('<provider>')` em fluxo user-facing; SDD lista s\u00f3 NOME da vault key | 0 viola\u00e7\u00f5es | grep audit + `/security-review` |\n| NFR-AC-013 | Security | RLS cross-tenant: tenant A nunca l\u00ea artefato biom\u00e9trico de B | 0 leaks | RLS test (FMEA-011) |\n| NFR-AC-014 | Privacy/Compliance | 100% dos renders/posts carregam C2PA machine-readable + disclosure deepfake (gate n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1vel) | 100% | Publish gate audit |\n| NFR-AC-015 | Privacy/Compliance | 100% das cria\u00e7\u00f5es de avatar com `avatar_consents` imut\u00e1vel + identity-verification passada | 100% | `avatar_consents` query |\n| NFR-AC-016 | **Identity Fidelity** | Identity Gate pass-rate na 1\u00aa gera\u00e7\u00e3o (limiar calibrado FAR/FRR/EER, N\u00c3O hardcode); dedu\u00e7\u00e3o SEMPRE at\u00f4mica via RPC `deduct_mco_coins`; zero dedu\u00e7\u00e3o client-side | 0 desvios | Ledger audit |\n| NFR-AC-020 | Observability | Cada path (success/degraded/error) emite telemetry em `infra_health_logs` (`service='avatar-clone-ai'`) + n\u00f3 de observa\u00e7\u00e3o na Knowledge Mesh no 1\u00ba sucesso | 100% dos paths | `infra_health_logs` query |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Interface Requirements\n\n### 4.1 UI \u2192 Hook \u2192 Edge Function Contract\n\n```\n[Component: AvatarStudioCanvas / BYOKSettingsCard / ConsentWizard]\n  \u2193 calls\n[Hook: useAvatarProviders / useAvatarConsent / useAvatarRender]\n  \u2193 calls (for sensitive ops)\n[Edge Function: generate-voice / canvas-execute (talkingHead) / erase-avatar-artifacts / avatar-reply-engine]\n  \u2193 reads/writes\n[Supabase Tables: user_api_keys (Vault) \u00b7 avatar_identities \u00b7 voice_profiles \u00b7 avatar_consents \u00b7 video_renders \u00b7 creative_assets \u00b7 channel_variants]\n```\n\nDisciplina Harness: componentes display-only; toda muta\u00e7\u00e3o/fetch nos hooks; sensitive ops (s\u00edntese, billing, erasure, reply) s\u00f3 em Edge Functions JWT-gated.\n\n### 4.2 Internal Edge Function Contracts\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/generate-voice` (FR-AC-009)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  script: string,            // input_script (texto do roteiro)\n  language: \"en\" | \"pt-BR\",  // idioma \u00fanico (nunca code-switch \u2014 FR-AC-010)\n  provider: \"elevenlabs\" | \"cartesia\",\n  voice_profile_id: string,  // refer\u00eancia a voice_profiles (owner-scoped)\n  project_id?: string,\n}\n```\n**Response (200):**\n```typescript\n{\n  audio_url: string,         // signed URL curta (bucket privado)\n  creative_asset_id: string, // registrado em creative_assets\n  mco_charged: number,       // ~36 (recalibr\u00e1vel)\n}\n```\n**Error codes:**\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 401 | Missing or invalid JWT |\n| 402 | `elevenlabs_not_configured` / `cartesia_not_configured` (BYOK fail-closed) OU saldo mcoCoins insuficiente |\n| 403 | Consentimento de voz revogado (fail-closed) ou sentinel block |\n| 422 | Valida\u00e7\u00e3o (idioma misto / script vazio \u2014 mensagem PT-BR no body) |\n| 500 | Internal error (logged to `infra_health_logs`) |\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/canvas-execute` (kind=`talkingHead`) (FR-AC-020/021)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  kind: \"talkingHead\",\n  input_face_identity: string,   // avatar_identities ref (owner-scoped)\n  input_voice_profile: string,   // audio asset ref\n  input_script: string,\n  source_type: \"footage_real\" | \"avatar_ai\",  // dirige roteamento de motor (FR-AC-022)\n  engine?: \"hedra\" | \"omnihuman\" | \"kling\",\n  reference_image_urls?: string[],\n  seed?: number,\n}\n```\n**Response (200 / async):**\n```typescript\n{\n  render_id: string,         // video_renders row\n  status: \"queued\",\n  transport: \"poll://\",      // webhook/poll async (NFR-AC-004)\n  mco_estimate: number,      // 480 Hedra / 2169 OmniHuman / 36 footage-real\n}\n```\n**Error codes:** 401 \u00b7 402 (`<engine>_not_configured` / saldo) \u00b7 403 (consent revogado / 100%-avatar sem footage real \u2014 FR-AC-033) \u00b7 422 \u00b7 500.\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/erase-avatar-artifacts` (FR-AC-027)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope) \u2014 tenant-guarded\n**Request:** `{ avatar_identity_id?: string, voice_profile_id?: string, reason:
